VIII.4
Overview of lattice QCD results for the quark-connected contribution

In this section, we briefly review a subset of the results obtained to date for
the hadronic-light-by-light contribution
a_{\mu}^{\rm HLbL}
using the
QED kernel derived above, restricting ourselves to the quark-connected contribution.
The relevant publications are
Chao:2020kwq
;
Chao:2021tvp
;
Chao:2022xzg
,
and some preliminary results can also be found in the earlier
proceedings contributions
Asmussen:2018oip
;
Asmussen:2019act
.
All these calculations have been performed on gauge ensembles provided by the
Coordinated Lattice Simulations (CLS) initiative
Bruno:2014jqa
. The ensembles were
generated using three flavours of non-perturbatively O(
a
)-improved
Wilson fermions and with the tree-level O(
a^{2}
)-improved Symanzik gauge action.

Reference
Chao:2020kwq
focuses on QCD with degenerate
u,d,s
quark,
corresponding to
m_{\pi}=m_{K}\simeq 420
MeV.
It contains results for the connected contribution
obtained either with Method 1 or Method 2.
Focussing first on Method 1 with the kernel choice
\overline{\cal L}^{(\Lambda=0.16)}
,
the difference in the integrand between choosing
a conserved or a local lattice vector current at vertex
x
was tested and found
to be modest
13
13

13

See Fig. 8 of Ref.
Chao:2020kwq
.
The difference is about 10% around the peak of the integrand, at
|y|\simeq 0.4
fm,
and not statistically significant for
|y|>0.6
fm.
. The cutoff effect on
a_{\mu}^{\rm HLbL}
for an ensemble with
lattice spacing of 0.076 fm turned out to be on the order of 10%,
with a significant uncertainty on this estimate.
Finite-size effects were probed directly by comparing
two ensembles differing only by their volume,
m_{\pi}L=4.4
and 6.4, and
their size found to be roughly consistent with the finite-size effects expected for the
\pi^{0}

pole contribution.
As for Method 2, the kernel
\overline{\cal L}^{(\Lambda=0.40)}
was found to be a good choice,
and the use of local and conserved currents was
investigated as well. The final choice fell on four local currents,
although the final integrand with respect to the variable
|y|
was found to be similar if the
current at vertex
z
was replaced by a conserved current.
The size of both cutoff effects and finite-size effects was similar to Method 1.
Hence, given the lower computational cost of Method 2, the latter method was selected
for all subsequent calculations.

Figure
13
shows the integrand for the
quark-connected contribution within Method 1 obtained in lattice QCD
with degenerate
u,d,s
quarks. It is compared to predictions based
on the calculations of section
VII
: the quark loop with
a ‘constituent mass’ of 350 MeV, the
\pi^{0}
and
\eta
pole
contributions, as well as the charged pion loop. For the latter two
contributions, a modification of the prediction applicable to the full
HLbL amplitude must be applied in order to account for the fact
that only the quark-connected contribution is considered. This leads
to an enhancement of the combined
\pi^{0}
and
\eta
contribution by a
factor of three
Bijnens:2016hgx
;
Gerardin:2017ryf
. The combined
contribution of pion and kaon loops, computed in the framework of
scalar QED, must instead be divided by a factor of
three
Chao:2020kwq
to match the contribution of quark-connected
diagrams. Figure
13
illustrates that a
semi-quantitative understanding of the integrand can be gained via
these fairly simple calculations.

Analogous plots (based on Method 2 with
\Lambda=0.40
) at lighter
pion masses can be found in
Chao:2021tvp
, for which similar
qualitative observations can be made. The
\pi^{0}
pole contribution becomes
increasingly dominant as the pion mass is lowered towards its physical
value. In the case of a heavy quark propagating in the
loop
Chao:2022xzg
, the upward trend as a function of the
lattice spacing is seen most clearly. The latter calculation allowed
for a state-of-the-art estimate of the charm-quark contribution to

a_{\mu}^{\rm HLbL}
.

Figure 13:
Integrand for the connected contribution using Method 1 with
\overline{\cal L}^{(\Lambda=0.16)}
on ensemble N202 of size
48^{3}\times 128
, lattice spacing 0.064 fm
Bruno:2016plf
with
m_{\pi}=m_{K}=421
MeV. The lattice data use a point-split current at
x
. The integrand is compared to the prediction for the pole contributions of the
\pi^{0}
and
\eta

mesons with a VMD transition form factor, which is expected to provide a good approximation to the tail. In addition, an attempt to describe the
short-distance contribution with a constituent-quark loop with a quark mass of 350 MeV is made. Figure reproduced from
Chao:2020kwq
.

IX
Conclusions

In this paper we have presented extensive details of our calculation
of the ‘QED kernel’ needed in the Lorentz-covariant coordinate-space
method for computing the hadronic light-by-light contribution to the
muon
(g-2)
in lattice QCD. At the core of this QED kernel is the
amplitude represented by the graph of
Fig. ​
3
. It is an unusual amplitude
in particle physics in that it involves both the plane-wave
propagation of the muon in the initial and final state, and the
emission by the muon of massless particles (photons) propagating to
definite coordinate-space positions. It is thereby a mixed
momentum-space and coordinate-space amplitude. We remark that such
mixed amplitudes, although quite complex, may have further interesting
applications in quantum-field theoretic calculations
Position_space_methods_new
;
Parrino_master_thesis
.

We were able to carry out the calculation analytically up to and
including the averaging over the direction of the muon
momentum. Numerical methods were used only in the final convolution
integral, which can be interpreted as yielding the static potential
generated by a certain (analytically known) electric charge
distribution in four space dimensions. While the angular integral of
this final convolution can perhaps still be handled analytically, and
we have made some progress in this direction
SchroederThesis
,
one angular integral still had to be performed numerically in our two
implementations of sections
IV
and

V
.

For the practical purpose of computing
a_{\mu}^{\rm HLbL}
on the
lattice, we have tested the robustness of the numerics by reproducing
a number of known light-by-light contributions, typically at the
one-percent level. Such a precision is sufficient for the foreseeable
future, given that the current precision goal for
a_{\mu}^{\rm HLbL}

is to reach the 10% level. As noted in the introduction, our
implementation of the QED kernel has already been applied in lattice
QCD calculations of
a_{\mu}^{\rm HLbL}

Chao:2020kwq
;
Chao:2021tvp
;
Chao:2022xzg
. Given the phenomenological
importance of the muon
(g-2)
, the present paper serves to document
and underpin a central aspect of these results.

The idea of treating photon propagators in the continuum,
infinite-volume theory has been applied in other contexts as well,
most recently in Ref.​
Biloshytskyi:2022ets
. One simpler
application is the fully covariant coordinate-space method for the HVP
contribution to the muon
(g-2)

Meyer:2017hjv
. Another
interesting application concerns the calculation of the QED
self-energy of stable hadrons without power-law finite-size
effects
Feng:2018qpx
. Further applications will probably follow
in the future.

Appendix A
The tensors
T_{\alpha\beta\delta}^{\rm A}(x,y)
in terms of the weight functions

In this appendix, we consider the weight functions
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}
,

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}

as being functions of
(|x|,\hat{c}_{\beta}\equiv\hat{x}\cdot\hat{y},|y|)

and use the notation
\partial^{[j]}
to denote the derivative of a weight function
with respect to its
j
’th argument.
The chain rule for
T^{({\rm I})}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)
reads

\displaystyle T^{{\rm I}}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)=\delta_{\beta\delta}\frac{x_{\alpha}}{|x|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big(\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big)\Big\}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\beta\delta}\frac{y_{\alpha}}{|x||y|}\Big\{\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+(\delta_{\alpha\delta}x_{\beta}+\delta_{\alpha\beta}x_{\delta})\frac{1}{|x|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\alpha\delta}\frac{y_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big\{\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\alpha\beta}\frac{y_{\delta}}{|x|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}x_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{x^{2}}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-2\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{|x|}\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{|x|}\Big(\frac{3\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}-\frac{1}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}y_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|\,y^{2}}\Big\{\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}-\frac{1}{|y|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}x_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|^{2}}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-2\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{|x|}\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\frac{1}{|x|}\Big(\frac{3\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}-\frac{1}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}y_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x|\,|y|^{2}}\Big\{\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}-\frac{1}{|y|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}x_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x|^{2}|y|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}-\frac{1}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}y_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x||y|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\frac{1}{|x|}\Big(\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}-\frac{1}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}y_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x||y|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\Big)\Big(\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{|x|}\Big(\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}-\frac{1}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}x_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|^{2}|y|}\Big\{\partial^{[1]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}+\Big(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\Big)\partial^{[2]}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}-\frac{1}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big\}.

(347)

Similarly, the chain rule for
T^{{\rm II}}_{\alpha\beta\delta}
and
T^{{\rm III}}_{\alpha\beta\delta}
read

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}T^{{\rm II}}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)=\delta_{\beta\delta}\frac{x_{\alpha}}{|x|}\left\{\frac{1}{4}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}\right)-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right)-\frac{|x||y|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{2}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right)-\frac{|x|}{2}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\beta\delta}\frac{y_{\alpha}}{|x||y|}\left\{\frac{1}{4}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}-\frac{|x||y|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{2}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}-\frac{|x||y|}{2}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left(\delta_{\alpha\beta}x_{\delta}+\delta_{\alpha\delta}x_{\beta}\right)\left\{\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left(\delta_{\alpha\beta}y_{\delta}+\delta_{\alpha\delta}y_{\beta}\right)\left\{\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}x_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}y_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x||y|}\left\{\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}x_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x||y|}\left\{\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}y_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}x_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}y_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}x_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x||y|}\left\{\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}y_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x||y|}\left\{\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

(348)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}T^{{\rm III}}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)=\left(\delta_{\beta\delta}x_{\alpha}+\delta_{\alpha\beta}x_{\delta}\right)\left\{\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left(\delta_{\beta\delta}y_{\alpha}+\delta_{\alpha\beta}y_{\delta}\right)\left\{\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\alpha\delta}\frac{x_{\beta}}{|x|}\left\{\frac{1}{4}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.-\frac{|x||y|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{2}\left(\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right)-\frac{|x|}{2}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\alpha\delta}\frac{y_{\beta}}{|y|}\left\{\frac{1}{4}\left(\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\left(\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\left(\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.-\frac{|x||y|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{2}\left(\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right)-\frac{|y|}{2}\left(\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}x_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}y_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|y|}\left\{\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}y_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|y|}\left\{\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}x_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}x_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}x_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|x|}\left\{\partial^{[1]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\left(\frac{1}{|y|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{y_{\alpha}y_{\beta}x_{\delta}}{|y|}\left\{\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{x_{\alpha}y_{\beta}y_{\delta}}{|y|}\left\{\partial^{[3]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\left(\frac{1}{|x|}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|y|}\right)\partial^{[2]}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\right\}.

(349)

Appendix B
Derivatives of the integrands for the six weight functions with respect to
|x|

In this appendix, we provide the expressions of the
|x|
-derivatives of the relevant sums
(see Eqs.
131
–
133
as well as Eqs.
220
)-(
227
)
entering the expression for the six weight functions parametrizing the QED kernel.

A few notational remarks are in order. In this appendix, we write the
argument of
z_{n}
as the four-vector
u
instead of
u^{2}
and use the
notation
z_{n}^{\prime}\equiv\frac{\partial}{\partial|u|}z_{n}
, not

\frac{\partial}{\partial|u|^{2}}
. Also, we set
v=x-u
. The argument
of the Gegenbauer polynomials
C_{n}
and their derivatives is always
(\hat{u}\cdot\widehat{x-u})
.

We obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial|x|}\sigma_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|u-x|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\,\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

(350)

\displaystyle+\frac{|u||x||\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}{|x-u|^{3}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}\;,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\sigma_{3}}{\partial|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{c}_{1}}{|x|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big\{z_{n}(u)\Big[-\frac{z_{n+1}(v)}{|x|}+\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}z_{n+1}^{\prime}(v)\Big]\frac{C_{n+1}}{n+2}

(351)

\displaystyle\qquad+z_{n+1}(u)\Big[-\frac{z_{n}(v)}{|x|}+\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\Big]\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}{|x-u|^{2}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big\{\Big(\Big[|u|\hat{c}_{1}-\frac{(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})}{n+1}\Big]\frac{z_{n}(u)z_{n+1}(v)}{|x-u|}+\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{n+1}z_{n}(u)z_{n+1}^{\prime}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n+1}^{\,\prime}}{n+2}

\displaystyle+\Big(\Big[|u|\hat{c}_{1}+\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{n+2}\Big]\frac{z_{n+1}(u)z_{n}(v)}{|x-u|}-\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{n+2}z_{n+1}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{|x||u|\hat{s}_{1}^{4}}{|x-u|^{4}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(n+1)(n+2)}\Big\{z_{n}(u)z_{n+1}(v)C^{\,\prime\prime}_{n+1}-z_{n+1}(u)z_{n}(v)C^{\,\prime\prime}_{n}\Big\},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial|x|}\sigma_{2}=-\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\{\frac{z_{n}(u)z_{n+1}^{\prime}(v)}{n+2}C_{n+1}+\frac{z_{n+1}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)}{n+1}C_{n}\right\}

(352)

\displaystyle+\frac{x^{2}\,|u|\hat{c}_{1}\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}{|x-u|^{4}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(n+1)(n+2)}\Big\{z_{n}(u)z_{n+1}(v)\,C_{n+1}^{\prime\prime}-z_{n+1}(u)z_{n}(v)C_{n}^{\prime\prime}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|x-u|^{3}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(n+1)(n+2)}\,\Big\{

\displaystyle\Big[|x||x-u|\hat{c}_{1}(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})z_{n+1}^{\prime}(v)+|u|\Big(|u|\hat{c}_{1}-|x|\hat{c}_{1}^{2}-(n+1)|x|\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\Big)z_{n+1}(v)\Big]z_{n}(u)C_{n+1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\Big[|x||x-u|\hat{c}_{1}(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})z_{n}^{\prime}(v)+|u|\Big(|u|\hat{c}_{1}-|x|\hat{c}_{1}^{2}+(n+2)|x|\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\Big)z_{n}(v)\Big]z_{n+1}(u)C_{n}^{\prime}\Big\},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial|x|}\sigma_{1}=-\frac{|x|}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

(353)

\displaystyle-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{u^{2}}{4}\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\Big(z_{n-2}(u)+(2-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(u)+z_{n+2}(u)\Big)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{(x-u)^{2}}{4}\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)\Big(z_{n-2}^{\prime}(v)+(2-\delta_{n0})z_{n}^{\prime}(v)+z_{n+2}^{\prime}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)\Big(z_{n-2}(v)+(2-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(v)+z_{n+2}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{|u|(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})}{4|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big\{\Big(z_{n}(u)z_{n+2}(v)+2z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)+z_{n+2}(u)z_{n}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n+1}}{n+2}

\displaystyle+\Big(z_{n-2}(u)z_{n}(v)+2z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)+z_{n}(u)z_{n-2}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n-1}}{n}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{|u|}{4}(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big\{\Big(z_{n}(u)z_{n+2}^{\prime}(v)+2z_{n}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)+z_{n+2}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n+1}}{n+2}

\displaystyle+\Big(z_{n-2}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)+2z_{n}(u)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)+z_{n}(u)z_{n-2}^{\prime}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n-1}}{n}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{|x||u|\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}{|x-u|^{3}}\bigg\{-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{u^{2}}{4}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(v)\Big(z_{n-2}(u)+(2-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(u)+z_{n+2}(u)\Big)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{(x-u)^{2}}{4}\sum_{n\geq 0}z_{n}(u)\Big(z_{n-2}(v)+(2-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(v)+z_{n+2}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{|u||x-u|}{4}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big\{\Big(z_{n}(u)z_{n+2}(v)+2z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)+z_{n+2}(u)z_{n}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n+1}^{\,\prime}}{n+2}

\displaystyle+\Big(z_{n-2}(u)z_{n}(v)+2z_{n}(u)z_{n}(v)+z_{n}(u)z_{n-2}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n-1}^{\,\prime}}{n}\Big\}\bigg\},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial s_{1}}{\partial|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{u^{2}}{4}\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(z_{n-2}(u)+(1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(u)+z_{n+2}(u)\Big)z_{n}^{\prime}(v)\,\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

(354)

\displaystyle+\frac{|u|^{3}|x|\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}{4|x-u|^{3}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(z_{n-2}(u)+(1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(u)+z_{n+2}(u)\Big)z_{n}(v)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}\;,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial s_{2}}{\partial|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)\Big(z_{n-2}(v)+(1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(v)+z_{n+2}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

(355)

\displaystyle+\frac{|x-u|(|x|-|u|\hat{c}_{1})}{4}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)\Big(z_{n-2}^{\prime}(v)+(1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}^{\prime}(v)+z_{n+2}^{\prime}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}}{n+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{|x||u|\hat{s}_{1}^{2}}{4|x-u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}z_{n}(u)\Big(z_{n-2}(v)+(1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}(v)+z_{n+2}(v)\Big)\frac{C_{n}^{\,\prime}}{n+1}\;,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\sigma_{4}}{\partial|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\Big(-\frac{2|u|}{x^{2}}\hat{c}_{1}\sigma_{1}+(2\frac{|u|}{|x|}\hat{c}_{1}-1)\frac{\partial\sigma_{1}}{\partial|x|}+\frac{\partial s_{2}}{\partial|x|}-\frac{\partial s_{1}}{\partial|x|}\Big),

(356)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\sigma_{5}}{\partial|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{u^{2}}\Big(\frac{|u|}{|x|^{3}}(-2|u|(1+2\hat{c}_{1}^{2})+3|x|\hat{c}_{1})\sigma_{1}+\frac{|u|}{x^{2}}(|u|(1+2\hat{c}_{1}^{2})-3|x|\hat{c}_{1})\frac{\partial\sigma_{1}}{\partial|x|}

(357)

\displaystyle-3\frac{|u|}{x^{2}}\hat{c}_{1}(s_{2}-s_{1})+3\frac{|u|}{|x|}\hat{c}_{1}(\frac{\partial s_{2}}{\partial|x|}-\frac{\partial s_{1}}{\partial|x|})+3\frac{\partial s_{1}}{\partial|x|}\Big).

Appendix C
Expansion of the kernel for small arguments

This appendix provides the relevant expressions to obtain the QED weight functions and the full kernel
at
x=0
or at
y=0
.
In the following, we refer to various functions defined mainly in subsection
V.2
.

C.1
The regime of small
|x|

In this subsection, the argument of the
z_{n}
is always
u^{2}
if not
explicitly specified. As in appendix
B
,

z_{n}^{\prime}
means
\frac{\partial}{\partial|u|}z_{n}
, not
the derivative with respect to
u^{2}
. As for the sums
\sigma_{k}

(
1\leq k\leq 5
), we recall that their generic arguments are

(|x|,\hat{c}_{1}\equiv\hat{x}\cdot\hat{u},|u|)
.

We begin by giving the first terms of the Taylor expansion of the sums
\sigma_{k}
that enter the calculation
of the QED weight functions
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}
,

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}
,
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}
. In several cases, we express the result in terms of auxiliary sums collected
below in Eqs. (
372
–
379
). We obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{0}\big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\,z_{n}^{2}\;,

(358)

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}\big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(1+\frac{2n}{3})\frac{z_{n}z_{n+1}}{|u|}~+~\hat{c}_{1}^{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Big(z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime}-z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime}\Big),

(359)

\displaystyle\lim_{|x|\to 0}\frac{\sigma_{1}}{x^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4\hat{c}_{1}^{2}-1}{12}\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u),

(360)

\displaystyle\lim_{|x|\to 0}\frac{\sigma_{2}}{|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{c}_{1}\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u),

(361)

\displaystyle\lim_{|x|\to 0}\frac{\sigma_{4}}{|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{3}\hat{c}_{1}\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\,\sigma^{(1,2)}_{4}(u),

(362)

\displaystyle\sigma_{5}\big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\hat{s}_{1}^{4}\sigma_{5}^{(0,2)}(u).

(363)

In addition, the following intermediate results are needed,

\displaystyle\lim_{|x|\to 0}\frac{s_{2}-s_{1}}{|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{|u|\hat{c}_{1}}{2}\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u),

(364)

\displaystyle s_{1}\big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{u^{2}}{4}\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u).

(365)

For some of the sums, we will need the expansion to one order higher.
In particular, we record

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}(s_{2}-s_{1})}{\partial|x|^{2}}\Big|_{x=0}=\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u)+\frac{|u|\hat{c}_{1}^{2}}{2}\sigma_{s_{1}}^{(1,1)}(u),

(366)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial s_{1}}{\partial|x|}\Big|_{x=0}=-\frac{\hat{c}_{1}u^{2}}{4}\,\sigma_{s_{1}}^{(1,1)}(u),

(367)

as well as the results

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\sigma_{3}}{\partial|x|}\Big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{c}_{1}^{3}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime\prime}-z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\right)

(368)

\displaystyle+\hat{c}_{1}\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\left(\frac{(2n+3)z_{n}z_{n+1}}{2u^{2}}-\frac{(2n+9)z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime}}{6|u|}+\frac{(3-2n)z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime}}{6|u|}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}\sigma_{1}}{\partial|x|^{3}}\Big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\Big(C_{1}(\hat{c}_{1})\,\hat{t}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)+C_{3}(\hat{c}_{1})\,\hat{u}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)\Big),

(369)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}\sigma_{2}}{\partial|x|^{2}}\Big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{1}^{4}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{(2n+3)z_{n}z_{n+1}}{6u^{2}}+\frac{z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime}-z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime}}{2|u|}\right)

(370)

\displaystyle+\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\hat{c}_{1}^{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\left(\frac{(2n+3)z_{n}z_{n+1}}{3u^{2}}-\frac{(n+3)z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime}}{3|u|}-\frac{nz_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime}}{3|u|}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\frac{z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime\prime}-z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\sigma_{5}}{\partial|x|}\Big|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\hat{c}_{1}\hat{s}_{1}^{4}}{3}\;\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u).

(371)

The auxiliary sums appearing in the results above are defined as follows,

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}z_{n}\Big((1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}+2z_{n+2}\Big)=3u^{2}b(u^{2}),

(372)

\displaystyle\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\left(z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime}-z_{n}z_{n+1}^{\prime}+(1+\frac{2n}{3})\,\frac{z_{n}z_{n+1}}{|u|}\right),

(373)

\displaystyle\sigma^{(1,2)}_{4}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u),\phantom{\frac{1}{1}}

(374)

\displaystyle\sigma_{5}^{(0,2)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u),\phantom{\frac{1}{1}}

(375)

\displaystyle\sigma_{s_{1}}^{(1,1)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Big[(1-\delta_{n0})z_{n}z_{n}^{\prime}+z_{n}z_{n+2}^{\prime}+z_{n+2}z_{n}^{\prime}\Big],

(376)

\displaystyle\hat{t}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24|u|}\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u)-\frac{1}{48}\sigma_{s_{1}}^{(1,1)}(u),

(377)

\displaystyle\hat{u}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{48}\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u),

(378)

\displaystyle\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u)}{|u|}-\sigma_{s_{1}}^{(1,1)}(u).

(379)

We recall that
b(u^{2})
was first introduced in Eqs. (
117
–
118
).

C.1.1
The scalar weight function

The behavior of
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}
around
x=0
is determined by
a small number of coefficients
\alpha^{(0)}_{m\pm}
; see Eq. (
211
).
All
\alpha^{(0)}_{m\pm}
vanish at
|x|=0
, except

\displaystyle\alpha_{0-}^{(0)}(0,|y|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\;|u|^{3}\;z_{n}^{2}

(380)

and
\alpha_{0+}^{(0)}
; the latter, whose integral is infrared divergent at large
|u|
, is however not needed.
As for the
|x|
-derivative of the coefficients at the origin, they all vanish except

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\alpha_{1+}^{(0)}}{\partial|x|}\Big|_{|x|=0}=\lim_{|x|\to 0}\alpha_{1+}^{(0)}(|x|,|y|)/|x|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\;z_{n}z_{n}^{\prime}\qquad

(381)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}z_{n}(y^{2})^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\alpha_{1-}^{(0)}}{\partial|x|}\Big|_{|x|=0}=\lim_{|x|\to 0}\alpha_{1-}^{(0)}(|x|,|y|)/|x|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\;|u|^{4}\;z_{n}z_{n}^{\prime}\qquad

(382)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{0-}^{(0)}(|x|=0,|y|)-|y|^{4}\lim_{|x|\to 0}\alpha_{1+}^{(0)}(|x|,|y|)/|x|.

Combining these observations, one finds for the actually needed derivatives of the scalar weight function

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial|x|}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}\right|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\hat{c}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{3}}\frac{\alpha^{(0)}_{1-}(|x|,|y|)}{|x|}+|y|\frac{\alpha^{(0)}_{1+}(|x|,|y|)}{|x|}\Big)_{x=0},

(383)

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}\right|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2|x|\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{3}}\frac{\alpha^{(0)}_{1-}(|x|,|y|)}{|x|}+|y|\frac{\alpha^{(0)}_{1+}(|x|,|y|)}{|x|}\Big)_{x=0}+{\rm O}(x^{2}),

(384)

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial|y|}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}\right|_{x=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{|y|^{3}}\alpha^{(0)}_{0-}(|x|=0,|y|).

(385)

From Eqs. (
383
–
384
) one sees that

\frac{\partial}{\partial|x|}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{0}-\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{|x|}\frac{\partial}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{0}={\rm O}(|x|).

This and further similar relations for the other weight functions will be exploited to arrive
at the final expressions of the tensors
T^{\rm A}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)
, Eqs. (
413
–
415
) below.

C.1.2
The vector weight functions

In the Taylor expansion of a QED weight function
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(k)}
, we denote by
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(k,n)}
the term of order
|x|^{n}
.
For
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}
, one finds
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2,0)}=0
and

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{|x|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{12}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{5}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\;|u|^{5}\;\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)+|y|\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|}\;\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)\Big],

(386)

and

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{x^{2}}{6}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{4}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{4}\sigma_{2}^{(2,1)}(u)+\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,\sigma_{2}^{(2,1)}(u)\Big]

(387)

\displaystyle-\frac{x^{2}}{30}(6\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}-1)\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{6}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)+|y|^{2}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\,\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)\Big],

\displaystyle\sigma_{2}^{(2,1)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{48u^{2}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Big\{|u|z_{n+1}\Big((2n+15)z_{n}^{\prime}+3|u|z_{n}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

(388)

\displaystyle\qquad+z_{n}\Big((9+6n)z_{n+1}+|u|((2n-9)z_{n+1}^{\prime}-3|u|z_{n+1}^{\prime\prime})\Big)\Big\},

\displaystyle\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{5}{96u^{2}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Big\{|u|z_{n+1}\Big((3-2n)z_{n}^{\prime}-3|u|z_{n}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

(389)

\displaystyle\qquad+z_{n}\Big((9+6n)z_{n+1}+|u|(3|u|z_{n+1}^{\prime\prime}-(9+2n)z_{n+1}^{\prime})\Big)\Big\}.

Similarly for
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(3)}
:

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(3,0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{2}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{3}\sigma_{3}^{(0,0)}(u)+\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,|u|\,\sigma_{3}^{(0,0)}(u)\Big]

(390)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}-1}{6}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{4}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{5}\,\sigma_{3}^{(0,2)}(u)+y^{2}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|}\,\sigma_{3}^{(0,2)}(u)\Big],

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}^{(0,0)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8|u|}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Big\{-|u|z_{n+1}z_{n}^{\prime}+z_{n}((2n+3)z_{n+1}+|u|z_{n+1}^{\prime})\Big\},

(391)

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}^{(0,2)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)

(392)

(with
\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)
defined in Eq. (
373
)), as well as

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(3,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{2}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{3}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{4}\,\sigma_{3}^{(1,1)}(u)+|y|\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,\sigma_{3}^{(1,1)}(u)\Big]

(393)

\displaystyle+\frac{|x|\hat{c}_{\beta}(2\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}-1)}{2}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{5}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\,\sigma_{3}^{(1,3)}(u)+|y|^{3}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\,\sigma_{3}^{(1,3)}(u)\Big],

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}^{(1,1)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{48u^{2}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Big\{|u|z_{n+1}((3-2n)z_{n}^{\prime}+3|u|z_{n}^{\prime\prime})

(394)

\displaystyle+z_{n}((6n+9)z_{n+1}-|u|((2n+9)z_{n+1}^{\prime}+3|u|z_{n+1}^{\prime\prime}))\Big\},

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}^{(1,3)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{5}\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)

(395)

(with
\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)
given in Eq. (
389
)).
These results imply, for the actually needed weight function
\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}
,

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1,0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{2}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{3}\sigma_{3}^{(0,0)}(u)+\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,|u|\,\sigma_{3}^{(0,0)}(u)\Big]

(396)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{48}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{4}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{5}\,\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)+y^{2}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|}\,\sigma_{2}^{(1,2)}(u)\Big],

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|x|\hat{c}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{3}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{4}\,(\sigma_{3}^{(1,1)}(u)+\frac{1}{3}\sigma_{2}^{(2,1)}(u))+|y|\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,(\sigma_{3}^{(1,1)}(u)+\frac{1}{3}\sigma_{2}^{(2,1)}(u))\Big)

(397)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{15}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{5}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\,\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)+|y|^{3}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\,\sigma_{2}^{(2,3)}(u)\Big)\Big].

C.1.3
The tensor weight functions

Similar to above, in the Laurent series of a QED weight function

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(k)}
, we denote by
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(k,n)}

the term of order
|x|^{n}
. First, using the same notation for the
auxiliary weight function
\bar{v}^{(1)}
, we find

\displaystyle\bar{v}^{(1,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{x^{2}(4\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}-1)}{72}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{4}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{5}\,\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u)+y^{2}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|}\,\sigma_{1}^{(2,2)}(u)\Big),

(398)

\displaystyle\bar{v}^{(1,3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|^{3}C_{1}(\hat{c}_{\beta})}{2}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{3}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{4}\,\hat{t}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)+|y|\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,\hat{t}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)\Big)

(399)

\displaystyle+\frac{|x|^{3}C_{3}(\hat{c}_{\beta})}{4}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{5}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\hat{u}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)+|y|^{3}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\,\hat{u}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u)\Big).

We can now proceed to determining the first two non-trivial terms for weight function
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(4)}
,

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(4,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|\,\hat{c}_{\beta}}{9}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{3}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{5}\,\sigma^{(1,2)}_{4}(u)+|y|^{3}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|}\;\sigma^{(1,2)}_{4}(u)\Big],

(400)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(4,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-x^{2}}{3}\Big(\frac{1}{y^{2}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{4}\sigma_{4}^{(2,1)}(u)+y^{2}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,\sigma_{4}^{(2,1)}(u)\Big)

(401)

\displaystyle-\frac{x^{2}(6\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}-1)}{15}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{4}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\,\sigma_{4}^{(2,3)}(u)+|y|^{4}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\sigma_{4}^{(2,3)}(u)\Big),

\displaystyle\sigma_{4}^{(2,1)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-4\hat{t}_{\sigma_{1}}^{(3,1)}(u),

(402)

\displaystyle\sigma_{4}^{(2,3)}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{24}\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u).

(403)

Next come the first two terms for weight function
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}
,

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2,0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{18}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{6}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{5}\,\sigma_{5}^{(0,2)}(u)+\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|}\,\sigma_{5}^{(0,2)}(u)\Big),

(404)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|\hat{c}_{\beta}}{24}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{7}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u)+|y|\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u)\Big).

(405)

Now from Eq. (
192
), we obtain
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}
via

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2x^{2}y^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(4)}-\frac{|y|}{|x|}\,\hat{c}_{\beta}\;\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)},

(406)

so that the
{\rm O}(1/|x|)
contribution cancels out,

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3,-1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2x^{2}y^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(4,1)}-\frac{|y|}{|x|}\,\hat{c}_{\beta}\;\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2,0)}=0.

(407)

The leading contribution is finite,

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3,0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2x^{2}y^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(4,2)}-\frac{|y|}{|x|}\,\hat{c}_{\beta}\;\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2,1)}

(408)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{6}\Big[\frac{1}{|y|^{4}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{4}\sigma_{4}^{(2,1)}(u)+\int_{|y|}^{\infty}d|u|\,\sigma_{4}^{(2,1)}(u)

(409)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{24}\Big(\frac{1}{|y|^{6}}\int_{0}^{|y|}d|u|\,|u|^{6}\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u)+y^{2}\int_{|y|}^{\infty}\frac{d|u|}{|u|^{2}}\sigma_{5}^{(1,3)}(u)\Big)\Big].

Similarly, one finds that the
{\rm O}(1/x^{2})
contribution to (see Eq. (
193
))

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}=\frac{4}{3x^{4}}\Big(\bar{v}_{1}-x^{2}y^{2}(\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}-\frac{1}{4})\;\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}-\frac{3}{2}x^{3}y\hat{c}_{\beta}\;\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\Big)

(410)

vanishes,

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-2)}=0.

(411)

For the contribution of order
1/|x|
, it is useful to decompose the
expression in the basis of the
C_{m}(\hat{c}_{\beta})
, calculating the coefficient

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-1,m)}
of
C_{m}(\hat{c}_{\beta})
using

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-1,m)}=\frac{2}{\pi}\int_{0}^{\pi}d\beta\,\sin^{2}\beta\,C_{m}(\hat{c}_{\beta})\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-1)}
.
Before forming the linear combination (
410
),
we find that there are
m=1
and
m=3
components, but they cancel in the linear combination,

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-1,1)}=\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-1,3)}=0
,
so that

\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1,-1)}=0.

(412)

Thus
\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}
is finite in the limit
|x|\to 0
.

C.1.4
The limit
|x|\to 0
for the tensors
T^{\rm A}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)

With the help of the chain rules for the tensors
T^{\rm A}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)
given in appendix
A
,
we find the following, finite expressions for these three tensors at
|x|\to 0
,

\displaystyle T^{\rm I}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(0,y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\delta_{\alpha\delta}\hat{y}_{\beta}+\delta_{\beta\delta}\hat{y}_{\alpha})\left(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\right)+\delta_{\alpha\beta}y_{\delta}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}}{\partial|x|^{2}}-{\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}}{x^{2}\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}}\right)

(413)

\displaystyle+\delta_{\alpha\delta}\hat{y}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{\partial|y|}\Big)+(\delta_{\beta\delta}\hat{y}_{\alpha}+\delta_{\alpha\beta}\hat{y}_{\delta})\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}}{|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\hat{y}_{\alpha}\hat{y}_{\beta}\hat{y}_{\delta}\;\frac{1}{|x|}\Big(\frac{|y|}{|x|}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}+|y|\frac{\partial}{\partial|y|}-1\Big)\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\,,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}T^{\rm II}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(0,y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\delta_{\alpha\beta}y_{\delta}+\delta_{\alpha\delta}y_{\beta}-\frac{y_{\alpha}}{2}\delta_{\beta\delta}\Big)\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}+\Big(y_{\beta}y_{\delta}-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\delta_{\beta\delta}\Big)\hat{y}_{\alpha}\left(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}}{|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\right)

(414)

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{\beta\delta}}{4}\hat{y}_{\alpha}\left(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}T^{\rm III}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(0,y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\delta_{\beta\alpha}y_{\delta}+\delta_{\beta\delta}y_{\alpha}-\frac{y_{\beta}}{2}\delta_{\alpha\delta}\Big)\Big(\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}+\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\Big)

(415)

\displaystyle+\Big(y_{\alpha}y_{\delta}-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\delta_{\alpha\delta}\Big)\hat{y}_{\beta}\left(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}}{|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}+\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(2)}}{\partial|y|}\right)+\frac{\delta_{\alpha\delta}}{4}\hat{y}_{\beta}\left(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}+\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial|y|}\right).

The right-hand side should be evaluated in the limit
|x|\to 0
.
Using the results respectively in Eqs. (
386
,
397
,

387
,
396
), (
408
,
405
,

384
) and (
404
,
408
,
405
,

384
,
385
) the evaluation of the three tensors is
easily performed. We remark that the
\hat{c}_{\beta}
dependence cancels everywhere,
in particular in the second term of Eq. (
413
), which contains an explicit factor of
\hat{c}_{\beta}^{2}
.

C.2
The regime of small
|y|

In the following, we give without further comment the relevant expressions for the weight functions in the limit of
|y|\to 0
.

C.2.1
The scalar weight function

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial|x|}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\,|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;\frac{\partial\sigma_{0}}{\partial|x|}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)=\frac{\partial\alpha_{0+}^{(0)}(|x|,0)}{\partial|x|},

(416)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|y|}{\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;C_{1}(\hat{c}_{1})\,\sigma_{0}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)=2|y|\alpha_{1+}^{(0)}(|x|,0),

(417)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial|y|}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{1}(\hat{c}_{\beta})\,\alpha_{1+}^{(0)}(|x|,0).\qquad

(418)

C.2.2
The vector weight functions

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\,|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;\sigma_{3}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)=\alpha^{(3)}_{0+}(|x|,0),

(419)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{3\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;\sigma_{2}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)=2\,\beta^{(2)}_{1+}(|x|,0),

(420)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{\partial|y|}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{1}(\hat{c}_{\beta})\Big[-\frac{1}{2|x|}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}\Big|_{y=0}+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;\hat{c}_{1}\;\sigma_{3}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)\Big]

(421)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{1}(\hat{c}_{\beta})\Big[-\frac{\beta^{(2)}_{1+}(|x|,0)}{|x|}+\alpha^{(3)}_{1+}(|x|,0)\Big].

(422)

C.2.3
The tensor weight functions

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{3\pi|x|^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\;|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;\sigma_{1}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)=\frac{4}{3|x|^{4}}\alpha^{(1)}_{0+}(|x|,0),

(423)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{3\pi\,x^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\;\sigma_{4}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)=\frac{\beta^{(4)}_{1+}(|x|,0)}{x^{2}},

(424)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}}{\partial|y|}\Big|_{y=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1}(\hat{c}_{\beta})}{|x|}\Big[\frac{4}{3\pi|x|^{3}}\int_{0}^{\infty}d|u|\int_{0}^{\pi}d\phi_{1}\;\hat{s}_{1}^{2}\,\hat{c}_{1}\;\sigma_{1}(|x|,\hat{c}_{1},|u|)-\;\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\Big|_{y=0}\Big]

(425)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{1}(\hat{c}_{\beta})\Big[\frac{4}{3|x|^{4}}\alpha^{(1)}_{1+}(|x|,0)-\frac{1}{|x|^{3}}\beta^{(4)}_{1+}(|x|,0)\Big].

(426)

C.2.4
The limit
|y|\to 0
for the tensors
T^{\rm A}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,y)

We find the finite result

\displaystyle T_{\alpha\beta\delta}^{\rm I}(x,0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\delta_{\alpha\delta}\hat{x}_{\beta}+\delta_{\beta\delta}\hat{x}_{\alpha}+\delta_{\alpha\beta}\hat{x}_{\delta}-\hat{x}_{\alpha}\hat{x}_{\beta}\hat{x}_{\delta}\Big)\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{\partial|x|}\Big)+\hat{x}_{\alpha}\hat{x}_{\beta}x_{\delta}\Big(\frac{\partial^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{\partial|x|^{2}}\Big)~~~~

(427)

\displaystyle+\Big(\delta_{\alpha\delta}\hat{x}_{\beta}+\delta_{\alpha\beta}\hat{x}_{\delta}-\hat{x}_{\alpha}\hat{x}_{\beta}\hat{x}_{\delta}\Big)\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{|y|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\Big)+\hat{x}_{\alpha}\hat{x}_{\beta}x_{\delta}\Big(\frac{\partial^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(1)}}{|y|\partial|x|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\delta_{\beta\delta}\,\hat{x}_{\alpha}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(2)}}{\partial|x|}\Big),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}T^{\rm II}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{\beta\delta}\,\hat{x}_{\alpha}}{4}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial|x|}\Big)

(428)

\displaystyle+\Big(\delta_{\alpha\beta}x_{\delta}+x_{\beta}\delta_{\alpha\delta}-\frac{x_{\alpha}}{2}\delta_{\beta\delta}\Big)\,\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\Big(x_{\beta}x_{\delta}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\delta_{\beta\delta}\Big)\,\hat{x}_{\alpha}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}}{\partial|x|}\Big),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}T^{\rm III}_{\alpha\beta\delta}(x,0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{\alpha\delta}}{4}\,\hat{x}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial|x|}+\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{|y|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\Big)

(429)

\displaystyle+\Big(\delta_{\alpha\beta}x_{\delta}+x_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta\delta}-\frac{x_{\beta}}{2}\delta_{\alpha\delta}\Big)\Big(\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}+\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(3)}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(x_{\alpha}x_{\delta}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\delta_{\alpha\delta}\Big)\hat{x}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}}{\partial|x|}+\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{l}}^{(1)}}{|y|\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}\Big),

where the right-hand side should be evaluated in the limit
|y|\to 0
.
Using the results above in this subsection, this evaluation is easily performed.

Appendix D
Contribution of the scalar function
S(x,y)
to the QED kernel: large-
|y|
asymptotics

The scalar weight function is given by

S(x,y)=\int_{u,{\rm IR-reg.}}G_{0}(y-u)s(x,u),

(430)

with
s(x,u)\sim|u|^{-2}
at large
|u|
, see Eq. (
101
).
Thus the
y
-dependence of
S(x,y)
corresponds to the static potential induced (in four space dimensions)
by a charge distribution given by
s(x,u)
,
x
playing the role of a fixed position vector.
The function
S(x,y)
itself is logarithmically infrared-divergent, however

-\frac{\partial}{\partial|y|}S(x,y)
, which corresponds to the radial electric field,
is finite.
The electric field generated by a charge distribution falling like
|u|^{-2}

is of order
|y|^{-1}
, in any number of dimensions greater than two.
This is easiest obtained by applying Gauss’ law to a sphere of radius
|y|
and one finds

-\frac{\partial}{\partial|y|}S(x,y)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle|y|\to\infty}}{{=}}\frac{1}{384\pi^{2}m^{2}|y|},

(431)

independent of
x
, which is kept fixed.
Although we have obtained this from the region of large
u
, it is clear that the integral over
|u|

from 0 to a finite
|u|_{\rm max}
cannot generate an electric field falling off as slowly as
|y|^{-1}
;
instead it generates an O(
|y|^{-3}
) field.

We now proceed to determining the leading behavior of
\frac{\partial}{\partial|x|}S(x,y)

and
\frac{\partial}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}S(x,y)
at large
|y|
.
For this purpose it is useful to write (similarly to Eq. (
116
))

s(x,u)-s(0,u)=\sum_{n\geq 0}(-1)^{n}z_{n}(u^{2})\Big[z_{n}((x-u)^{2})(-1)^{n}\frac{C_{n}(\hat{u}\cdot\widehat{x-u})}{n+1}-z_{n}(u^{2})\Big],

(432)

and to realize that the expression inside the square bracket has the asymptotic large-
|u|
behavior

\frac{\hat{u}\cdot x}{4\pi^{2}m\,(n+1)u^{2}}
.
The series is then still absolutely convergent and one finds

s(x,u)-s(0,u)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle|u|\to\infty}}{{=}}\frac{1}{192\pi^{2}m^{2}|u|^{3}}\,{x\cdot\hat{u}}.

(433)

Thus we get, using Eq. (
430
) and the multipole expansion (
66
–
67
)
of the photon propagator
G_{0}(y-u)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial|x|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{c}_{\beta}}{768\pi^{2}m^{2}|y|},

(434)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{g}}^{(0)}}{\partial\hat{c}_{\beta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|x|}{768\pi^{2}m^{2}|y|}.

(435)

Therefore the scalar contribution to the tensors
T_{\alpha\beta\delta}^{\rm II}(x,y)
and

T_{\alpha\beta\delta}^{\rm III}(x,y)
is respectively

\displaystyle\frac{m}{4}\delta_{\beta\delta}\partial_{\alpha}^{(x)}S(x,y)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle|y|\to\infty}}{{=}}\frac{\delta_{\beta\delta}\,\hat{y}_{\alpha}}{3072\pi^{2}m|y|}(1+{\rm O}((m|y|)^{-1})),

(436)

\displaystyle\frac{m}{4}\delta_{\alpha\delta}(\partial_{\beta}^{(x)}+\partial_{\beta}^{(y)})S(x,y)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle|y|\to\infty}}{{=}}\frac{-\delta_{\alpha\delta}\,\hat{y}_{\beta}}{3072\pi^{2}m|y|}(1+{\rm O}((m|y|)^{-1})).

(437)

We note that this result is consistent with the scalar contribution to the rank-three tensors
satisfying Eq. (
50
).

Appendix E
Our version of the kernel code

Our implementation of the QED kernel
KQED
can be found in
KQEDcode
, it is licensed under version 3 of the GNU public license
GPLv3
. The library is built using GNU automake and is intended to be linked as a static library. An example for integrating the lepton loop using
hcubature

cubature
can be found in the companion code
KAMU

KAMUcode
, which illustrates how to link to
KQED
and initialize it.

KQED
includes a look-up-table of the Chebyshev coefficients as a file in single precision, which is entirely read upon initialization (and the crc32c of it is computed for correctness), although all computations are performed in double precision. The code can be compiled with
OpenMP
to use multi-threading whereby the kernel at coordinates
x
and
y
can be called safely within a parallel region. The code makes heavy use of AVX/FMA intrinsics to speed up the calculation of the various terms
S(x,y),V_{\delta}(x,y),
and
T_{\beta\delta}(x,y)
, particularly in the Clenshaw recurrences (Eqs. (
231
), (
232
) and (
233
)). Where possible, loop-fusion is performed on all necessary weight-function derivatives (see Sec.
VI
), as well as an internal re-mapping of neighboring elements on the grid to SIMD lanes.

The code compiles a binary which performs some simple unit tests and provides a stress-test for the time taken to compute a fixed number of expensive kernel calls. A script is also included in the package to regression-test the kernel against various
x
and
y
, and to check the multi-threading equivalence. Heavily loop-unrolled and optimised versions of the subtracted kernels (with 4 arbitrary
\Lambda
terms Eq. (
VIII.1
)) are available as this was one of the most costly parts of our calculation in
Chao:2020kwq
;
Chao:2021tvp
and
Chao:2022xzg
.
